{{}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} {}\lxSVG@fillstroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 3.11 0 C 3.11 1.72 1.72 3.11 0 3.11 C -1.72 3.11 -3.11 1.72 -3.11 0 C -3.11 -1.72 -1.72 -3.11 0 -3.11 C 1.72 -3.11 3.11 -1.72 3.11 0 Z M 0 0}{} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
\lxSVG@closescope }{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-1.20082pt}{-1.7361pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{60}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
}
\lxSVG@closescope {{{}}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}^{i}\alpha_{ef}^{\hbox to4.9pt{\vbox to4.9pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\>\lower-2.44878pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {
{{}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} {}\lxSVG@fillstroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 3.11 0 C 3.11 1.72 1.72 3.11 0 3.11 C -1.72 3.11 -3.11 1.72 -3.11 0 C -3.11 -1.72 -1.72 -3.11 0 -3.11 C 1.72 -3.11 3.11 -1.72 3.11 0 Z M 0 0}{} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
\lxSVG@closescope }{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-1.20082pt}{-1.7361pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{60}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
}
\lxSVG@closescope {{{}}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}>=\sum_{ef}<\alpha_{\hbox to4.9pt{\vbox to4.9pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\>\lower-2.44878pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {
{{}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} {}\lxSVG@fillstroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 3.11 0 C 3.11 1.72 1.72 3.11 0 3.11 C -1.72 3.11 -3.11 1.72 -3.11 0 C -3.11 -1.72 -1.72 -3.11 0 -3.11 C 1.72 -3.11 3.11 -1.72 3.11 0 Z M 0 0}{} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
\lxSVG@closescope }{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-1.20082pt}{-1.7361pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{60}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
}
\lxSVG@closescope {{{}}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}^{ij}\alpha_{e}^{f\hbox to4.9pt{\vbox to4.9pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\>\lower-2.44878pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {
{{}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} {}\lxSVG@fillstroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 3.11 0 C 3.11 1.72 1.72 3.11 0 3.11 C -1.72 3.11 -3.11 1.72 -3.11 0 C -3.11 -1.72 -1.72 -3.11 0 -3.11 C 1.72 -3.11 3.11 -1.72 3.11 0 Z M 0 0}{} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
\lxSVG@closescope }{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-1.20082pt}{-1.7361pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{60}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
}
\lxSVG@closescope {{{}}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}>=\sum_{ef}<\alpha_{i}^{j\hbox to4.9pt{\vbox to4.9pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\>\lower-2.44878pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {
{{}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} {}\lxSVG@fillstroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 3.11 0 C 3.11 1.72 1.72 3.11 0 3.11 C -1.72 3.11 -3.11 1.72 -3.11 0 C -3.11 -1.72 -1.72 -3.11 0 -3.11 C 1.72 -3.11 3.11 -1.72 3.11 0 Z M 0 0}{} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
\lxSVG@closescope }{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-1.20082pt}{-1.7361pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{60}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
}
\lxSVG@closescope {{{}}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\alpha_{\hbox to4.9pt{\vbox to4.9pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\>\lower-2.44878pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {
{{}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} {}\lxSVG@fillstroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 3.11 0 C 3.11 1.72 1.72 3.11 0 3.11 C -1.72 3.11 -3.11 1.72 -3.11 0 C -3.11 -1.72 -1.72 -3.11 0 -3.11 C 1.72 -3.11 3.11 -1.72 3.11 0 Z M 0 0}{} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
\lxSVG@closescope }{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-1.20082pt}{-1.7361pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{60}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
}
\lxSVG@closescope {{{}}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}^{ef}>=0.

(50)

Using the expectation identities, explicitly adding in the complex conjugate term, and dropping the low-order terms yields

\displaystyle s\mathcal{L}[n_{{{\bf k}}}](s)-n_{{{\bf k}}}(0)\approx

\displaystyle\mu^{2}\sum_{1,2}\Bigg[\frac{2n_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}\delta_{12}^{0}}{(s+\iota\Omega_{12}^{0})s}-\frac{4n_{{{\bf k}}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}\delta_{12}^{0}}{(s+\iota\Omega_{12}^{0})s}+\frac{4n_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}\delta_{1}^{20}}{(s+\iota\Omega_{1}^{20})s}-\frac{4n_{{{\bf k}}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}\delta_{1}^{20}}{(s+\iota\Omega_{1}^{20})s}+\frac{4n_{{{\bf k}}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}\delta_{1}^{20}}{(s+\iota\Omega_{1}^{20})s}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad-\frac{4n_{{{\bf k}}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}\delta_{1}}{(s+\iota\Omega_{1})s}+\frac{4n_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}\delta^{0}}{(s+\iota\Omega^{0})s}+\frac{4n_{{{\bf k}}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}\delta^{2}}{(s+\iota\Omega^{2})s}

\displaystyle+\frac{2n_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}\delta_{12}^{0}}{(s-\iota\Omega_{12}^{0})s}-\frac{4n_{{{\bf k}}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}\delta_{12}^{0}}{(s-\iota\Omega_{12}^{0})s}+\frac{4n_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}\delta_{1}^{20}}{(s-\iota\Omega_{1}^{20})s}-\frac{4n_{{{\bf k}}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}\delta_{1}^{20}}{(s-\iota\Omega_{1}^{20})s}+\frac{4n_{{{\bf k}}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}\delta_{1}^{20}}{(s-\iota\Omega_{1}^{20})s}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad-\frac{4n_{{{\bf k}}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}\delta_{1}}{(s-\iota\Omega_{1})s}+\frac{4n_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}\delta^{0}}{(s-\iota\Omega^{0})s}+\frac{4n_{{{\bf k}}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}\delta^{2}}{(s-\iota\Omega^{2})s}\Bigg].

(51)

Upon collecting like terms and simplifying we obtain

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}[n_{{{\bf k}}}](s)-\frac{n_{{{\bf k}}}(0)}{s}\approx

\displaystyle\mu^{2}\sum_{1,2}\Bigg[\delta_{12}^{0}\frac{4n_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}-8n_{{{\bf k}}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}}{s(s^{2}+(\Omega_{12}^{0})^{2})}+\delta_{1}^{20}\frac{8n_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}-8n_{{{\bf k}}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}+8n_{{{\bf k}}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}}{s(s^{2}+(\Omega_{1}^{20})^{2})}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad-\frac{8n_{{{\bf k}}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}\delta_{1}}{s(s^{2}+(\Omega_{1})^{2})}+\frac{8n_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}\delta^{0}}{s(s^{2}+(\Omega^{0})^{2})}+\frac{8n_{{{\bf k}}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}\delta^{2}}{s(s+(\Omega^{2})^{2})}\Bigg].

(52)

Now taking the inverse Laplace transform yields

\displaystyle n_{{{\bf k}}}(t)-n_{{{\bf k}}}(0)\approx

\displaystyle\mu^{2}\sum_{1,2}\Bigg[\delta_{12}^{0}\left(4n_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}-8n_{{{\bf k}}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}\right)\frac{1-\cos(t\Omega_{12}^{0})}{(\Omega_{12}^{0})^{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\delta_{1}^{20}\left(8n_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}-8n_{{{\bf k}}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}+8n_{{{\bf k}}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}\right)\frac{1-\cos(t\Omega_{1}^{20})}{(\Omega_{1}^{20})^{2}}

\displaystyle-4n_{{{\bf k}}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}\delta_{1}\frac{1-\cos(t\Omega_{1})}{\Omega_{1}}+8n_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}\delta^{0}\frac{1-\cos(t\Omega^{0})}{\Omega^{0}}+8n_{{{\bf k}}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}\delta^{2}\frac{1-\cos(t\Omega^{2})}{\Omega^{2}}\Bigg].

(53)

This can be more compactly written as

\displaystyle n_{{{\bf k}}}(t)-n_{{{\bf k}}}(0)\approx

\displaystyle\mu^{2}\sum_{1,2}\Bigg[\delta_{12}^{0}\left(2n_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}-4n_{{{\bf k}}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}\right)g(t,\Omega_{12}^{0}))

\displaystyle\qquad+\delta_{1}^{20}\left(4n_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}-4n_{{{\bf k}}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}+4n_{{{\bf k}}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}\right)g(t,\Omega_{1}^{20})

\displaystyle-2n_{{{\bf k}}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}\delta_{1}g(t,\Omega_{1})+4n_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}\delta^{0}g(t,\Omega^{0})+4n_{{{\bf k}}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}\delta^{2}g(t,\Omega^{2})\Bigg],

(54)

where

g\left(t,\Omega\right)=\frac{\sin^{2}(\frac{t\Omega}{2})}{\left(\frac{\Omega}{2}\right)^{2}}.

Now note that

\displaystyle\sum_{1,2}\delta_{12}^{0}f({{\bf k}}_{1},{{\bf k}}_{2},{{\bf k}})

\displaystyle=\sum_{{{\bf j}}_{1}}\sum_{{{\bf j}}_{2}}\delta[{{\bf k}}_{1}+{{\bf k}}_{2}-{{\bf k}}]f({{\bf k}}_{1},{{\bf k}}_{2},{{\bf k}})

(55a)

\displaystyle=\sum_{{{\bf j}}_{1}}f({{\bf k}}_{1},{{\bf k}}-{{\bf k}}_{1},{{\bf k}})

(55b)

\displaystyle=\sum_{{{\bf j}}_{1}}\int\delta({{\bf k}}_{1}+{{\bf k}}_{2}-{{\bf k}})f({{\bf k}}_{1},{{\bf k}}_{2},{{\bf k}})\,d{{\bf k}}_{2},

(55c)

where the convolution sum with a Kronecker delta has been replaced with a convolution integral with the Dirac delta. As a result, we can write

\displaystyle n_{{{\bf k}}}(t)-n_{{{\bf k}}}(0)\approx

\displaystyle\mu^{2}\sum_{{{\bf j}}_{1}}\int\delta({{\bf k}}_{1}+{{\bf k}}_{2}-{{\bf k}})\left(2n_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}-4n_{{{\bf k}}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}\right)g(t,\Omega_{12}^{0})

\displaystyle\qquad+\delta({{\bf k}}_{1}-{{\bf k}}_{2}-{{\bf k}})\left(4n_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}-4n_{{{\bf k}}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}+4n_{{{\bf k}}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}\right)g(t,\Omega_{1}^{20})

\displaystyle-2n_{{{\bf k}}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}\delta({{\bf k}}_{1})g(t,\Omega_{1})+4n_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}\delta({{\bf k}})g(t,\Omega^{0})+4n_{{{\bf k}}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}\delta({{\bf k}}_{2})g(t,\Omega^{2})\,d{{\bf k}}_{2}.

(56)

The dependence on
L
can be exposed in the formulation by unrolling the definition of
\mu
to give

\displaystyle n_{{{\bf k}}}(t)-n_{{{\bf k}}}(0)\approx

\displaystyle\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{L^{2}}\sum_{{{\bf j}}_{1}}\int\delta({{\bf k}}_{1}+{{\bf k}}_{2}-{{\bf k}})\left(2n_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}-4n_{{{\bf k}}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}\right)g(t,\Omega_{12}^{0})

\displaystyle\qquad+\delta({{\bf k}}_{1}-{{\bf k}}_{2}-{{\bf k}})\left(4n_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}-4n_{{{\bf k}}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}+4n_{{{\bf k}}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}\right)g(t,\Omega_{1}^{20})\,d{{\bf k}}_{2}

\displaystyle-2n_{{{\bf k}}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}\delta({{\bf k}}_{1})g(t,\Omega_{1})+4n_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}\delta({{\bf k}})g(t,\Omega^{0})+4n_{{{\bf k}}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}\delta({{\bf k}}_{2})g(t,\Omega^{2})\,d{{\bf k}}_{2}.

(57)

Because the k-space increments are given by
\Delta k=2\pi/L
, we now obtain

\displaystyle n_{{{\bf k}}}(t)-n_{{{\bf k}}}(0)\approx

\displaystyle\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\frac{(2\pi)^{2}}{L^{2}}\sum_{{{\bf j}}_{1}}\int\delta({{\bf k}}_{1}+{{\bf k}}_{2}-{{\bf k}})\left(2n_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}-4n_{{{\bf k}}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}\right)g(t,\Omega_{12}^{0})

\displaystyle\qquad+\delta({{\bf k}}_{1}-{{\bf k}}_{2}-{{\bf k}})\left(4n_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}-4n_{{{\bf k}}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}+4n_{{{\bf k}}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}\right)g(t,\Omega_{1}^{20})\,d{{\bf k}}_{2}

\displaystyle-2n_{{{\bf k}}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}\delta({{\bf k}}_{1})g(t,\Omega_{1})+4n_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}\delta({{\bf k}})g(t,\Omega^{0})+4n_{{{\bf k}}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}\delta({{\bf k}}_{2})g(t,\Omega^{2})\,d{{\bf k}}_{2},

(58)

and taking the limit as
L\to\infty
gives

\displaystyle n_{{{\bf k}}}(t)-n_{{{\bf k}}}(0)\approx

\displaystyle\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\int\int\delta({{\bf k}}_{1}+{{\bf k}}_{2}-{{\bf k}})\left(2n_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}-4n_{{{\bf k}}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}\right)g(t,\Omega_{12}^{0})

\displaystyle\qquad+\delta({{\bf k}}_{1}-{{\bf k}}_{2}-{{\bf k}})\left(4n_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}-4n_{{{\bf k}}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}+4n_{{{\bf k}}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}\right)g(t,\Omega_{1}^{20})\,d{{\bf k}}_{2}

\displaystyle-2n_{{{\bf k}}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}\delta({{\bf k}}_{1})g(t,\Omega_{1})+4n_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}\delta({{\bf k}})g(t,\Omega^{0})+4n_{{{\bf k}}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}\delta({{\bf k}}_{2})g(t,\Omega^{2})\,d{{\bf k}}_{2}\,d{{\bf k}}_{1}.

(59)

Taking
t\to\infty
, and relying on the following

\displaystyle\lim_{t\to\infty}=g(t,\Omega)=\lim_{t\to\infty}\frac{\sin^{2}\left(\frac{t\Omega}{2}\right)}{\left(\frac{\Omega}{2}\right)^{2}}=2\pi t\delta(\Omega)

(60)

leads to

\displaystyle n_{{{\bf k}}}(t)-n_{{{\bf k}}}(0)\approx

\displaystyle\frac{t\epsilon^{2}}{2\pi}\int\int\delta({{\bf k}}_{1}+{{\bf k}}_{2}-{{\bf k}})\left(2n_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}-4n_{{{\bf k}}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}\right)\delta(\Omega_{12}^{0})

\displaystyle\qquad+\delta({{\bf k}}_{1}-{{\bf k}}_{2}-{{\bf k}})\left(4n_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}-4n_{{{\bf k}}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}+4n_{{{\bf k}}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}\right)\delta(\Omega_{1}^{20})

\displaystyle-2n_{{{\bf k}}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}\delta({{\bf k}}_{1})\delta(\Omega_{1})+4n_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}\delta({{\bf k}})\delta(\Omega^{0})+4n_{{{\bf k}}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}\delta({{\bf k}}_{2})\delta(\Omega^{2})\,d{{\bf k}}_{2}\,d{{\bf k}}_{1}.

(61)

Here we see that the “funny” matching terms eventually drop out since
\Omega_{j}>0
and
\Omega^{j}>0
. Finally taking
\tau=t\epsilon^{2}
, and considering small
\tau
yields

\displaystyle\partial_{\tau}n_{{{\bf k}}}\approx

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\pi}\int\int\delta({{\bf k}}_{1}+{{\bf k}}_{2}-{{\bf k}})\left(n_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}-2n_{{{\bf k}}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}\right)\delta(\omega_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}+\omega_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}-\omega_{{{\bf k}}})

\displaystyle\qquad+\delta({{\bf k}}_{1}-{{\bf k}}_{2}-{{\bf k}})\left(2n_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}-2n_{{{\bf k}}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}+2n_{{{\bf k}}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}\right)\delta(\omega_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}-\omega_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}-\omega_{{{\bf k}}})\,d{{\bf k}}_{2}\,d{{\bf k}}_{1}.

(62)

Also if symmetry hadn’t been used in going from (
41
) to (
42
), the following more symmetric version results

\displaystyle\partial_{\tau}n_{{{\bf k}}}\approx

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\pi}\int\int\delta({{\bf k}}_{1}+{{\bf k}}_{2}-{{\bf k}})\left(n_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}-n_{{{\bf k}}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}-n_{{{\bf k}}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}\right)\delta(\omega_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}+\omega_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}-\omega_{{{\bf k}}})

\displaystyle\qquad+\delta({{\bf k}}_{1}-{{\bf k}}_{2}-{{\bf k}})\left(2n_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}-2n_{{{\bf k}}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}+2n_{{{\bf k}}}n_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}\right)\delta(\omega_{{{\bf k}}_{1}}-\omega_{{{\bf k}}_{2}}-\omega_{{{\bf k}}})\,d{{\bf k}}_{2}\,d{{\bf k}}_{1}.

(63)
